# HHWS-03 — Proposed documentation migration crosswalk

## Current delivery scope — supersedes earlier audit stop/push wording

User assignment **HHWS-DOCS-REBUILD-01 — PHASE 0 + PUSH** authorizes exactly these six reports, commit message `docs(audit): inventory backend and documentation for HHWS` and normal push to `origin/huy-review`. Product/actor decisions supplied by owner are preserved. No permission to edit production, migrations, contracts/OpenAPI, tests or existing docs; no Phase1 authorization. The request lists six output files although its return checklist says five; follow the explicit six-file Output list. No extra file staged. Earlier audit/handoff and five loose reports remain untouched and untracked outside this commit.

Delivery baseline after fetch: local HEAD and origin/huy-review equal `6fdd98d98b41311e3af07a7b8cb95c55d8d62bc5`, ahead/behind0/0. Source SHA in evidence remains this baseline; documentation-only delivery commit SHA will be verified after commit/push rather than embedded circularly in itself. No merge/rebase/reset/force push.

Status vocabulary requested by latest task: **CURRENT_VERIFIED** = source observed at pinned SHA or exact executed discovery/check; execution extent stated separately. **DOCUMENTED_ONLY** = old document assertion without current source/runtime proof; **PROPOSED**, **NOT_VERIFIED**, **HISTORICAL** preserve their meanings. OWNER_CONFIRMED = business authority; SOURCE_VERIFIED maps to CURRENT_VERIFIED/source only; RUNTIME_VERIFIED maps to CURRENT_VERIFIED/executed within stated test scope. Neither implies actual Web/Expo/FastAPI E2E.

Ngày 14/10/2026 · HoangHaiWarrantySystem (RoadGuardSystem = legacy technical identifier). **Phase 0 only — inventory/audit, không migration docs hoặc source edits.**

Branch `huy-review`; base/HEAD `6fdd98d98b41311e3af07a7b8cb95c55d8d62bc5`. Pre-existing untracked files: `docs/audit/RG-REPORTER-ANDROID-AUDIT-01.md`, `docs/frontend/REPORTER-EXPO-HANDOFF.md`; preserved. Root Codex là writer của đúng6báo cáo HHWS Phase0; không production/test/contract edits hoặc DB writes; latest task authorizes report-only commit/push.

Legend: OWNER_CONFIRMED = quyết định owner/task mới; SOURCE_VERIFIED = implementation ở HEAD đã đọc/hash đối chiếu; RUNTIME_VERIFIED = executed command/output trong scope cụ thể; DOCUMENT_STALE/PRODUCT_SOURCE_GAP/CONTRACT_GAP tách riêng. UNKNOWN/NOT_VERIFIED/PROPOSED không là PASS. SOURCE_VERIFIED không thay business authority hoặc actual client deployment.

## J — Verdict: PROPOSED canonical migration; no moves performed

Canonical root proposed by owner input: `docs/HoangHaiWarrantySystem-Official/`. Phase0 recommends this entrance with precedence and source/version/evidence legend. Existing docs remain unchanged; no old URL redirects, lock updates, mass copy of generated schemas or file deletion. Every crosswalk row is a **candidate destination**, not permission to adopt historical business/wire or automatically merge duplicates.

## Canonical topic ownership and import rules

| Destination | Input / rule | Ownership/status |
|---|---|---|
| README +00_Governance | Latest owner naming/client/scope + confirmed decisions + assigned spec; precedence and change log | Owner-confirmed, docs writer controlled |
|01_Product-and-Roles | New Mobile Expo/Web actor matrix; no iOS production claim; phiếu unavailable | Owner product authority |
|02_Business-Workflows | Current source seams + verified runtime and localized gates; one canonical workflow definition | Owner business + source evidence |
|03_Backend-and-Data | Existing source/EF/DI/security/storage model; preserve technical names | SOURCE_VERIFIED; no schema change |
|04_API-Contracts | HHWS-01 + current generated snapshot, exact version/route/headers/errors/consumer registry | Source-backed; adoption delta separately approved |
|05_Web | Supervisor/PM screen map; bearer/cookie transport eligibility exact; no primary ReporterWeb | Consumer UX PROPOSED until actual frontend source checked |
|06_Mobile-Expo | Reporter online, Operator survey, Crew FIELD/offline distinct; local/native encryption proposals | Stack OWNER_CONFIRMED; implementation NOT_VERIFIED |
|07_AI-FastAPI | Actual legacy callback and synthetic path separately; proposed real provider cookbook with gaps | AI repo/checkpoint/E2E NOT_VERIFIED |
|08_Testing-and-Operations | Fresh commands/counters/failures; historic acceptance separated; deployment/source-data gates | RUNTIME_VERIFIED only within run scope |
|99_Archive | Links/history source-index, do not migrate payload source copies automatically | HISTORICAL; retain original identifiers/paths |

## Migration controls after approval

Phase1 only: governance/README/precedence/source registry + canonical pointers; no bulk migration. Preserve old paths/external links, explicit superseded labels instead of deleting historical source. Phase2–4 import workflow/backend/API and consumer sections by assigned scope; phase5 link/consistency QA before archive decisions. Require one writer/file; API schema generation references current source, not V2 drafts or Postman subset. Preserve current UTF8 generation and no invented operationId.

No previous acceptance changed by this crosswalk; root rules and owner business precedence remain. Review canonical directory and import policy once at HHWS-04; no per-file approval loop.
